{
  "command": "python3 << 'PYEOF'\nimport json\n\n# Load new results\nwith open('/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0514_4/projects/distrib_canary_collapse/artifacts/conditional_granger_v2.json') as f:\n    new = json.load(f)\n\nresults = new['results']\ndownstreams = ['distinct_1', 'distinct_2', 'distinct_3', 'mauve']\n\nfor alpha_label, alpha in [(\"α=0.05\", 0.05), (\"α=0.10\", 0.10)]:\n    counts = {'entropy_only': 0, 'ece_only': 0, 'both_independent': 0, 'redundant': 0, 'neither': 0}\n    total_pairs = 0\n    \n    for exp_name, exp_data in results.items():\n        for ds in downstreams:\n            if ds not in exp_data['pairs']:\n                continue\n            pair = exp_data['pairs'][ds]\n            total_pairs += 1\n            \n            # lag=1 conditional p-values\n            p_ent = pair['cond_entropy_given_ece']['1']['p_value']\n            p_ece = pair['cond_ece_given_entropy']['1']['p_value']\n            \n            ent_sig = p_ent < alpha\n            ece_sig = p_ece < alpha\n            \n            if ent_sig and ece_sig:\n                counts['both_independent'] += 1\n            elif ent_sig and not ece_sig:\n                counts['entropy_only'] += 1\n            elif not ent_sig and ece_sig:\n                counts['ece_only'] += 1\n            else:\n                # Neither conditional is significant — check unconditional\n                p_ent_uncond = pair['uncond_entropy']['1']['p_value']\n                p_ece_uncond = pair['uncond_ece']['1']['p_value']\n                if p_ent_uncond < alpha or p_ece_uncond < alpha:\n                    counts['redundant'] += 1\n                else:\n                    counts['neither'] += 1\n    \n    print(f\"\\n=== {alpha_label} (N={len(results)} experiments, {total_pairs} pairs) ===\")\n    for k, v in counts.items():\n        pct = v / total_pairs * 100\n        print(f\"  {k:20s}: {v:3d} ({pct:5.1f}%)\")\n\n# Partial R² summary\nprint(\"\\n=== Partial R² (lag=1) ===\")\nent_pr2_list = []\nece_pr2_list = []\nfor exp_name, exp_data in results.items():\n    for ds in downstreams:\n        if ds not in exp_data['pairs']:\n            continue\n        pair = exp_data['pairs'][ds]\n        # Compute partial R² from F-stats and df\n        for test_key, pr2_list in [('cond_entropy_given_ece', ent_pr2_list), ('cond_ece_given_entropy', ece_pr2_list)]:\n            f = pair[test_key]['1']['F_stat']\n            df = pair[test_key]['1']['df']\n            df1, df2 = df[0], df[1]\n            pr2 = (df1 * f) / (df1 * f + df2)\n            pr2_list.append(pr2)\n\nimport statistics\nprint(f\"  Entropy|ECE: mean={statistics.mean(ent_pr2_list):.4f}, median={statistics.median(ent_pr2_list):.4f}\")\nprint(f\"  ECE|Entropy: mean={statistics.mean(ece_pr2_list):.4f}, median={statistics.median(ece_pr2_list):.4f}\")\nprint(f\"  Entropy > ECE in {sum(1 for e, c in zip(ent_pr2_list, ece_pr2_list) if e > c)}/{len(ent_pr2_list)} pairs\")\n\n# Breakdown by experiment category\nprint(\"\\n=== Dominance by category (α=0.05) ===\")\ncategories = {\n    'alpha_sweep': [k for k in results if k.startswith('a') and not k.startswith('a0') or k.startswith('a0')],\n    'C4': [k for k in results if k.startswith('c4_')],\n    'generalize': [k for k in results if k.startswith(('pythia', 'qwen', 'smollm'))],\n    'T=20': [k for k in results if k.startswith('t20_')],\n}\n# Fix alpha_sweep to exclude others\ncategories['alpha_sweep'] = [k for k in results if not k.startswith(('c4_', 'pythia', 'qwen', 'smollm', 't20_'))]\n\nfor cat_name, cat_keys in categories.items():\n    cat_counts = {'entropy_only': 0, 'ece_only': 0, 'both': 0, 'redundant': 0, 'neither': 0}\n    n_pairs = 0\n    for exp_name in cat_keys:\n        exp_data = results[exp_name]\n        for ds in downstreams:\n            if ds not in exp_data['pairs']:\n                continue\n            pair = exp_data['pairs'][ds]\n            n_pairs += 1\n            p_ent = pair['cond_entropy_given_ece']['1']['p_value']\n            p_ece = pair['cond_ece_given_entropy']['1']['p_value']\n            if p_ent < 0.05 and p_ece < 0.05:\n                cat_counts['both'] += 1\n            elif p_ent < 0.05:\n                cat_counts['entropy_only'] += 1\n            elif p_ece < 0.05:\n                cat_counts['ece_only'] += 1\n            else:\n                p_eu = pair['uncond_entropy']['1']['p_value']\n                p_cu = pair['uncond_ece']['1']['p_value']\n                if p_eu < 0.05 or p_cu < 0.05:\n                    cat_counts['redundant'] += 1\n                else:\n                    cat_counts['neither'] += 1\n    print(f\"\\n  {cat_name} ({len(cat_keys)} exps, {n_pairs} pairs):\")\n    for k, v in cat_counts.items():\n        print(f\"    {k:15s}: {v:2d} ({v/n_pairs*100:.0f}%)\")\nPYEOF\n",
  "description": "Compute dominance patterns and partial R² from new results"
}

{
  "server": "westd-19828",
  "command": "source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base && python3 << 'PYEOF'\nimport json\nwith open('/root/autodl-tmp/distrib_canary_collapse/results/analysis/conditional_granger_v2.json') as f:\n    old = json.load(f)\nprint(f\"Old experiments ({len(old['results'])}): {sorted(old['results'].keys())}\")\ndownstreams = ['distinct_1', 'distinct_2', 'distinct_3', 'mauve']\nfor alpha_label, alpha in [(\"alpha=0.05\", 0.05), (\"alpha=0.10\", 0.10)]:\n    counts = {'entropy_only': 0, 'ece_only': 0, 'both_independent': 0, 'redundant': 0, 'neither': 0}\n    total_pairs = 0\n    for exp_name, exp_data in old['results'].items():\n        for ds in downstreams:\n            if ds not in exp_data.get('pairs', {}):\n                continue\n            pair = exp_data['pairs'][ds]\n            total_pairs += 1\n            p_ent = pair['cond_entropy_given_ece']['1']['p_value']\n            p_ece = pair['cond_ece_given_entropy']['1']['p_value']\n            ent_sig = p_ent < alpha\n            ece_sig = p_ece < alpha\n            if ent_sig and ece_sig:\n                counts['both_independent'] += 1\n            elif ent_sig and not ece_sig:\n                counts['entropy_only'] += 1\n            elif not ent_sig and ece_sig:\n                counts['ece_only'] += 1\n            else:\n                p_eu = pair['uncond_entropy']['1']['p_value']\n                p_cu = pair['uncond_ece']['1']['p_value']\n                if p_eu < alpha or p_cu < alpha:\n                    counts['redundant'] += 1\n                else:\n                    counts['neither'] += 1\n    print(f\"\\n{alpha_label} ({total_pairs} pairs):\")\n    for k, v in counts.items():\n        pct = v / total_pairs * 100\n        print(f\"  {k:20s}: {v:3d} ({pct:5.1f}%)\")\nPYEOF\n"
}

_source line 391 · 2026-05-21T04:57:15.070Z_

All data collected. Here's the Step 7 comparison:

---

## OLD vs NEW Conditional Granger Causality — Comparison

### Coverage
| | OLD | NEW |
|---|---|---|
| Experiments | 11 (wrong paths, non-fp32, some nonexistent) | 31 (correct fp32 paths) |
| Pairs | 44 | 124 |
| Categories | α∈{0.50,0.55,0.75} + 3 generalize | α∈{0.00–1.00} × multi-seed + C4 + generalize + T=20 |

### Dominance Patterns (α=0.05)
| Pattern | OLD (44 pairs) | NEW (124 pairs) | Shift |
|---|---|---|---|
| entropy_only | 4 (9.1%) | 28 (22.6%) | **+13.5pp** |
| ece_only | 11 (25.0%) | 18 (14.5%) | **−10.5pp** |
| both_independent | 12 (27.3%) | 41 (33.1%) | +5.8pp |
| redundant | 14 (31.8%) | 22 (17.7%) | **−14.1pp** |
| neither | 3 (6.8%) | 15 (12.1%) | +5.3pp |

### Key Takeaways

1. **ECE dominance was an artifact of biased sampling.** Old data over-represented high-α experiments (0.50, 0.55, 0.75) where entropy and ECE are highly correlated → inflated "ece_only" and "redundant" counts. Adding low-to-mid α experiments (0.00–0.45) where the two canaries decorrelate reveals entropy as the stronger independent predictor.

2. **Entropy > ECE in partial R².** Entropy|ECE has mean pR²=0.34 vs ECE|Entropy mean pR²=0.25; entropy wins in 65/124 (52%) pairs. Not a landslide — both carry information — but entropy has the edge when conditioned out.

3. **"Redundant" drops dramatically** (31.8% → 17.7%). With correct fp32 data at lower α values, the two canaries are more separable — the apparent collinearity in the old data was driven by the high-α regime.

4. **Category breakdown:**
   - **Alpha sweep**: entropy_only (26%) > ece_only (13%), redundant still sizable (26%) at high α
   - **C4 corpus**: both_independent dominates (50%) — entropy and ECE carry orthogonal info on C4
   - **Generalize** (Pythia, Qwen, SmolLM): both_independent (50%), consistent with canonical results
   - **T=20**: both_independent (45%), entropy_only = ece_only (25% each) — longer sequences bring out clearer separation
